// packages/plugin-sdk/src/context.d.ts
import type { EventTopic, EventsApiVersion } from "@scalius/shared/events";
import type { PluginSettingValue } from "@scalius/shared/plugins";
import type { PluginCustomerRole, PluginCustomerRoleGrant } from "@scalius/shared/plugins/customer-roles";
import type { PluginJob } from "@scalius/shared/plugins/jobs";
import type { PluginUpload, PluginUploadSlice } from "@scalius/shared/plugins/uploads";
export type { PluginCustomerRole, PluginCustomerRoleGrant } from "@scalius/shared/plugins/customer-roles";
export type { PluginJob, PluginJobStatus, PluginJobStepInput, PluginJobStepResult } from "@scalius/shared/plugins/jobs";
export type { PluginUpload, PluginUploadSlice } from "@scalius/shared/plugins/uploads";
export interface PluginStoreInfo {
    name: string;
    url: string | null;
    currency: string;
    decimalPlaces: number;
    timezone: string;
    locale: string;
}
export interface PluginApiCallInput {
    path?: Record<string, string | number>;
    query?: Record<string, string | number | boolean | null | undefined>;
    body?: unknown;
    idempotencyKey?: string;
}
export interface PluginApiResult<T = unknown> {
    ok: boolean;
    status: number;
    data: T | null;
    error: {
        code: string;
        message: string;
    } | null;
}
export interface PluginApi {
    call<T = unknown>(operation: string, input?: PluginApiCallInput): Promise<PluginApiResult<T>>;
}
export interface PluginMoney {
    amountMinor: number;
    currency: string;
}
export interface PluginObjectQuery {
    where?: Record<string, string | number | boolean | null | PluginMoney>;
    orderBy?: {
        field: string;
        direction?: "asc" | "desc";
    };
    limit?: number;
    cursor?: string | null;
}
export interface PluginRecordVersion<T extends Record<string, unknown> = Record<string, unknown>> {
    data: T;
    revision: number;
    handle: string | null;
    status: "active" | "draft" | "published";
}
export interface PluginWriteCondition {
    expectedRevision?: number | null;
}
export interface PluginObjectCollection<T extends Record<string, unknown> = Record<string, unknown>> {
    get(id: string): Promise<T | null>;
    getByHandle(handle: string): Promise<(PluginRecordVersion<T> & {
        id: string;
    }) | null>;
    getVersion(id: string): Promise<PluginRecordVersion<T> | null>;
    put(id: string, record: T, options?: PluginWriteCondition & {
        handle?: string | null;
        status?: "active" | "draft" | "published";
    }): Promise<{
        revision: number;
    }>;
    update(id: string, patch: Partial<T>, options?: {
        expectedRevision?: number;
    }): Promise<PluginRecordVersion<T>>;
    increment(id: string, field: string, delta: number, options?: {
        min?: number;
        max?: number;
    }): Promise<{
        value: number;
        revision: number;
    }>;
    delete(id: string, options?: PluginWriteCondition): Promise<boolean>;
    query(query?: PluginObjectQuery): Promise<{
        records: Array<PluginRecordVersion<T> & {
            id: string;
        }>;
        nextCursor: string | null;
    }>;
    count(where?: PluginObjectQuery["where"]): Promise<number>;
}
export type PluginObjectOperation = {
    op: "put";
    object: string;
    id: string;
    record: Record<string, unknown>;
    expectedRevision?: number | null;
    handle?: string | null;
    status?: "active" | "draft" | "published";
} | {
    op: "delete";
    object: string;
    id: string;
    expectedRevision?: number | null;
} | {
    op: "check";
    object: string;
    id: string;
    expectedRevision: number | null;
};
export type PluginObjects = Record<string, PluginObjectCollection> & {
    batch(operations: PluginObjectOperation[]): Promise<{
        revisions: Array<number | null>;
    }>;
};
export interface PluginKv {
    get<T = unknown>(key: string): Promise<T | null>;
    getVersion<T = unknown>(key: string): Promise<{
        value: T;
        revision: number;
    } | null>;
    set(key: string, value: unknown, options?: {
        ttlSeconds?: number;
    }): Promise<{
        revision: number;
    }>;
    compareAndSet(key: string, expectedRevision: number | null, value: unknown, options?: {
        ttlSeconds?: number;
    }): Promise<{
        revision: number;
    }>;
    delete(key: string): Promise<void>;
    list(prefix?: string): Promise<string[]>;
}
export interface PluginHttpResponse {
    status: number;
    ok: boolean;
    headers: Record<string, string>;
    text(): Promise<string>;
    json<T = unknown>(): Promise<T>;
}
export interface PluginHttp {
    fetch(url: string, init?: {
        method?: string;
        headers?: Record<string, string>;
        body?: string;
        timeoutMs?: number;
    }): Promise<PluginHttpResponse>;
}
export interface PluginCustomFields {
    get(ownerType: string, ownerId: string): Promise<Record<string, unknown>>;
    set(ownerType: string, ownerId: string, values: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void>;
}
export interface PluginMessaging {
    email(input: {
        customerId?: string;
        to?: string;
        subject: string;
        html: string;
        text?: string;
        marketing?: boolean;
        idempotencyKey?: string;
    }): Promise<{
        queued: boolean;
        duplicate?: boolean;
    }>;
    sms(input: {
        customerId?: string;
        to?: string;
        text: string;
        marketing?: boolean;
        idempotencyKey?: string;
    }): Promise<{
        queued: boolean;
        duplicate?: boolean;
    }>;
    notifyStaff(input: {
        title: string;
        body?: string;
        url?: string;
    }): Promise<void>;
    pushCustomer(input: {
        customerId: string;
        title: string;
        body?: string;
        url?: string;
        tag?: string;
    }): Promise<{
        delivered: boolean;
    }>;
}
export interface PluginTimeline {
    add(input: {
        orderId: string;
        kind: string;
        message: string;
        fields?: Record<string, string | number | boolean>;
        idempotencyKey?: string;
    }): Promise<{
        id: string;
    }>;
}
export interface PluginOrders {
    hold(input: {
        orderId: string;
        reason: string;
    }): Promise<void>;
    release(input: {
        orderId: string;
        expectedRevision: number;
    }): Promise<{
        revision: number;
        replayed: boolean;
    }>;
    assessRisk(input: {
        orderId: string;
        level: "low" | "medium" | "high";
        reasons: string[];
    }): Promise<void>;
    riskHistory(orderId: string): Promise<{
        orders: number;
        cancelled: number;
        returnedToSender: number;
    }>;
}
export interface PluginCustomerRoles {
    grant(customerId: string, role: string, options?: {
        ref?: string | null;
    }): Promise<void>;
    revoke(customerId: string, role: string): Promise<void>;
    list(customerId: string): Promise<PluginCustomerRoleGrant[]>;
}
export interface PluginCustomers {
    roles: PluginCustomerRoles;
}
export interface PluginJobs {
    start(name: string, options?: {
        input?: unknown;
        key?: string;
    }): Promise<{
        id: string;
        status: PluginJob["status"];
    }>;
    get(id: string): Promise<PluginJob | null>;
    list(options?: {
        name?: string;
        limit?: number;
    }): Promise<PluginJob[]>;
    cancel(id: string): Promise<PluginJob>;
}
export interface PluginUploads {
    get(id: string): Promise<PluginUpload | null>;
    read(id: string, options?: {
        offset?: number;
        length?: number;
    }): Promise<PluginUploadSlice>;
    delete(id: string): Promise<boolean>;
}
export interface PluginLogger {
    debug(message: string, fields?: Record<string, unknown>): void;
    info(message: string, fields?: Record<string, unknown>): void;
    warn(message: string, fields?: Record<string, unknown>): void;
    error(message: string, fields?: Record<string, unknown>): void;
}
export interface PluginContext {
    plugin: {
        id: string;
        version: string;
    };
    store: PluginStoreInfo;
    settings: Readonly<Record<string, PluginSettingValue>>;
    features: readonly string[];
    api: PluginApi;
    objects: PluginObjects;
    kv: PluginKv;
    http: PluginHttp;
    customFields: PluginCustomFields;
    messaging: PluginMessaging;
    timeline: PluginTimeline;
    orders: PluginOrders;
    customers: PluginCustomers;
    jobs: PluginJobs;
    uploads: PluginUploads;
    log: PluginLogger;
}
export interface PluginEvent<T extends EventTopic = EventTopic> {
    id: string;
    topic: T;
    apiVersion: EventsApiVersion;
    occurredAt: string;
    resource: {
        type: string;
        id: string;
    };
    facts: Record<string, unknown>;
}
export interface PluginRouteRequest {
    method: string;
    subpath: string;
    query: Record<string, string>;
    headers: Record<string, string>;
    body: unknown;
    rawBody: string | null;
    staffId: string | null;
    customer?: {
        id: string;
        roles: PluginCustomerRole[];
    } | null;
    routes?: PluginStorefrontRoutes | null;
}
export interface PluginStorefrontRoutes {
    readonly home: string;
    readonly search: string;
    readonly cart: string;
    readonly account: string;
    readonly signIn: string;
    readonly orders: string;
    readonly trackOrder: string;
}
export interface PluginRouteResponse {
    status?: number;
    headers?: Record<string, string>;
    body?: unknown;
}

import type { PluginEvent, PluginMoney, PluginRouteRequest } from "./context";
declare function event<T extends EventTopic>(topic: T, overrides?: Partial<PluginEvent<T>>): PluginEvent<T>;
declare function cartLine(overrides?: Partial<FunctionCart["lines"][number]>): FunctionCart["lines"][number];
declare function cart(overrides?: Partial<FunctionCart>): FunctionCart;
declare function checkoutDiscountsInput(overrides?: {
    cart?: Partial<FunctionCart>;
    reads?: FunctionReads;
}): PluginFunctionInput<"checkout.discounts">;
declare function money(amountMinor: number, currency?: string): PluginMoney;
declare function readRecord<T extends Record<string, unknown>>(data: T, revision?: number): {
    revision: number;
    data: T;
};
declare function adminRouteRequest(overrides?: Partial<PluginRouteRequest>): PluginRouteRequest;

    cart?: Partial<FunctionCart> | null;
};
declare function storefrontExtensionInput(target: StorefrontUiSlot, overrides?: StorefrontExtensionInputOverrides): BuyerUiInput;
type DeepPartial<T> = T extends readonly (infer U)[] ? U[] : T extends object ? {
    [K in keyof T]?: DeepPartial<T[K]> | null;
} : T;
declare function buyerEvent<N extends PixelEventName>(name: N, data?: DeepPartial<BuyerEventData[N]>, overrides?: Partial<Omit<BuyerEvent<N>, "name" | "data">>): BuyerEvent<N>;

    cart: typeof cart;

};

export type PluginEventHandler<T extends EventTopic = EventTopic, Ctx = PluginContext> = (event: PluginEvent<T>, ctx: Ctx) => Promise<void> | void;
export type PluginFunctionHandler<P extends PluginFunctionPoint, Settings = Readonly<Record<string, PluginSettingValue>>> = (input: PluginFunctionRunInput<P>, settings: Settings) => PluginFunctionAnswer<P> | Promise<PluginFunctionAnswer<P>>;
export type PluginExtensionHandler<Settings = Readonly<Record<string, PluginSettingValue>>> = (input: PluginFunctionRunInput<"buyer.ui">, settings: Settings) => PluginFunctionAnswer<"buyer.ui"> | Promise<PluginFunctionAnswer<"buyer.ui">>;
export type { FunctionReadRecord, FunctionReads, PluginFunctionRunInput };
export declare function readOne<T extends Record<string, unknown> = Record<string, unknown>>(reads: FunctionReads | undefined, as: string): {
    revision: number;
    data: T;
} | null;
export declare function readMany<T extends Record<string, unknown> = Record<string, unknown>>(reads: FunctionReads | undefined, as: string): Record<string, {
    revision: number;
    data: T;
}>;
export declare function readList<T extends Record<string, unknown> = Record<string, unknown>>(reads: FunctionReads | undefined, as: string): Array<{
    id: string;
    revision: number;
    data: T;
}>;
export type PluginRouteHandler<Ctx = PluginContext> = (request: PluginRouteRequest, ctx: Ctx) => Promise<PluginRouteResponse> | PluginRouteResponse;
export type PluginCronHandler<Ctx = PluginContext> = (ctx: Ctx, run: {
    name: string;
    scheduledAt: string;
}) => Promise<void> | void;
export type PluginLifecycleHandler<Ctx = PluginContext> = (ctx: Ctx) => Promise<void> | void;
export type PluginJobHandler<Ctx = PluginContext> = (ctx: Ctx, job: PluginJobStepInput) => Promise<PluginJobStepResult | void> | PluginJobStepResult | void;
export interface PluginDefinition<M extends PluginManifestInput = PluginManifestInput, Ctx = PluginContextOf<M>> {
    manifest: M;
    events?: {
        [T in EventTopic]?: PluginEventHandler<T, Ctx>;
    } & {
        [F in Exclude<EventTopicFilter, EventTopic>]?: PluginEventHandler<EventTopic, Ctx>;
    };
    functions?: {
        [P in PluginFunctionPoint]?: PluginFunctionHandler<P, PluginSettingsOf<M>>;
    };
    extensions?: {
        [T in StorefrontUiSlot]?: PluginExtensionHandler<PluginSettingsOf<M>>;
    };
    routes?: {
        admin?: Record<string, PluginRouteHandler<Ctx>>;
        public?: Record<string, PluginRouteHandler<Ctx>>;
        webhooks?: Record<string, PluginRouteHandler<Ctx>>;
    };
    cron?: Record<string, PluginCronHandler<Ctx>>;
    jobs?: Record<string, PluginJobHandler<Ctx>>;
    providers?: Record<string, PluginProvider>;
    lifecycle?: {
        install?: PluginLifecycleHandler<Ctx>;
        activate?: PluginLifecycleHandler<Ctx>;
        deactivate?: PluginLifecycleHandler<Ctx>;
        uninstall?: (ctx: Ctx, options: {
            keepData: boolean;
        }) => Promise<void> | void;
        enable?: (ctx: Ctx, options: {
            feature: string;
        }) => Promise<void> | void;
        disable?: (ctx: Ctx, options: {
            feature: string;
        }) => Promise<void> | void;
    };
    migrations?: Record<string, PluginMigrationHandler<Ctx>>;
}
export type PluginMigrationHandler<Ctx = PluginContext> = (ctx: Ctx) => Promise<void> | void;
export declare function definePlugin<const M extends PluginManifestInput, const T extends PluginDefinition<M>>(definition: T & {
    manifest: M;
}): T;

// packages/plugin-sdk/src/manifest-types.d.ts
import type { PluginManifestInput, PluginSettingValue } from "@scalius/shared/plugins";
import type { StorefrontImage, StorefrontProductCard, StorefrontResolvedSettings } from "@scalius/shared/plugins/storefront-protocol";
import type { PluginContext, PluginMoney, PluginObjectCollection, PluginObjects } from "./context";
type Manifest = PluginManifestInput;
declare const DEFINED_MANIFEST: unique symbol;
export type DefinedManifest<M> = M & {
    readonly [DEFINED_MANIFEST]?: true;
};
type IsDefined<M> = typeof DEFINED_MANIFEST extends keyof M ? true : false;
type Values<T> = T extends readonly (infer U)[] ? U : never;
type IsLiteral<K> = K extends string ? (string extends K ? false : true) : false;
export type ObjectFieldValue<Type> = Type extends `list.${infer Item}` ? Array<ObjectFieldValue<Item>> : Type extends "integer" | "decimal" ? number : Type extends "boolean" ? boolean : Type extends "json" ? unknown : Type extends "money" ? PluginMoney : Type extends string ? string : unknown;
type FieldOf<O> = O extends {
    fields?: infer F;
} ? Values<F> : never;
type RequiredKeys<F> = F extends {
    key: infer K extends string;
    required: true;
} ? K : never;
type OptionalKeys<F> = F extends {
    key: infer K extends string;
} ? (F extends {
    required: true;
} ? never : K) : never;
type FieldType<F, K> = F extends {
    key: K;
    type: infer T;
} ? ObjectFieldValue<T> : never;
export type ObjectRecordOf<O> = IsLiteral<FieldOf<O> extends {
    key: infer K;
} ? K : string> extends true ? {
    [K in RequiredKeys<FieldOf<O>>]: FieldType<FieldOf<O>, K>;
} & {
    [K in OptionalKeys<FieldOf<O>>]?: FieldType<FieldOf<O>, K> | null;
} : Record<string, unknown>;
type ObjectsOf<M> = M extends {
    objects?: infer O;
} ? NonNullable<O> : never;
export type PluginObjectsOf<M> = [
    ObjectsOf<M>
] extends [never] ? PluginObjects : string extends keyof ObjectsOf<M> ? PluginObjects : {
    readonly [Name in keyof ObjectsOf<M>]: PluginObjectCollection<ObjectRecordOf<ObjectsOf<M>[Name]>>;
} & PluginObjects;
type OptionValue<F> = F extends {
    options: infer O;
} ? (Values<O> extends {
    value: infer V;
} ? V : string) : string;
export type SettingValueOf<F> = F extends {
    type: "number";
} ? number : F extends {
    type: "boolean";
} ? boolean : F extends {
    type: "select";
} ? OptionValue<F> : F extends {
    type: "multiselect";
} ? Array<OptionValue<F>> : F extends {
    type: "image";
} ? StorefrontImage | null : F extends {
    type: "product";
} ? StorefrontProductCard | null : F extends {
    type: "products";
} ? StorefrontProductCard[] : F extends {
    type: "list";
    fields: infer Items;
} ? Array<{
    [I in Values<Items> as I extends {
        key: infer K extends string;
    } ? K : never]: SettingItemValue<I>;
}> : string;
type SettingItemValue<I> = I extends {
    type: "number";
} ? number | null : I extends {
    type: "boolean";
} ? boolean | null : I extends {
    type: "image";
} ? StorefrontImage | null : string | null;
type FeatureGroups<M> = M extends {
    features?: infer F;
} ? (Values<NonNullable<F>> extends {
    settingsGroups?: infer G;
} ? Values<NonNullable<G>> : never) : never;
type Always<F, M> = F extends {
    default: unknown;
} ? (F extends {
    group: FeatureGroups<M>;
} ? false : true) : false;
export type SettingsOf<Fields, M = unknown> = IsLiteral<Values<Fields> extends {
    key: infer K;
} ? K : string> extends true ? {
    readonly [F in Values<Fields> as F extends {
        key: infer K extends string;
    } ? (Always<F, M> extends true ? K : never) : never]: SettingValueOf<F>;
} & {
    readonly [F in Values<Fields> as F extends {
        key: infer K extends string;
    } ? (Always<F, M> extends true ? never : K) : never]?: SettingValueOf<F> | null;
} : unknown;
export type PluginSettingsOf<M> = IsDefined<M> extends true ? (M extends {
    settings?: infer S;
} ? SettingsOf<NonNullable<S>, M> : unknown) & Readonly<Record<string, PluginSettingValue>> : Readonly<Record<string, PluginSettingValue>>;
export type PluginContextOf<M> = IsDefined<M> extends true ? Omit<PluginContext, "objects" | "settings"> & {
    objects: PluginObjectsOf<M>;
    settings: PluginSettingsOf<M>;
} : PluginContext;
type StorefrontOf<M> = M extends {
    storefront?: infer S;
} ? NonNullable<S> : never;
type Declared<List, Id extends string, Key extends string> = Values<List> extends infer E ? (E extends {
    [P in Id]: Key;
} ? E : never) : never;
type SettingsOfEntry<E> = (E extends {
    settings?: infer S;
} ? SettingsOf<NonNullable<S>> : unknown) extends infer R ? (unknown extends R ? StorefrontResolvedSettings : R) : never;
export type StorefrontSettingsOf<M, Kind extends "section" | "component" | "skin", Key extends string> = Kind extends "section" ? SettingsOfEntry<Declared<StorefrontOf<M> extends {
    sections?: infer L;
} ? NonNullable<L> : never, "type", Key>> : Kind extends "component" ? SettingsOfEntry<Declared<StorefrontOf<M> extends {
    components?: infer L;
} ? NonNullable<L> : never, "type", Key>> : SettingsOfEntry<Declared<StorefrontOf<M> extends {
    skins?: infer L;
} ? NonNullable<L> : never, "id", Key>>;
export declare function defineManifest<const M extends Manifest>(manifest: M): DefinedManifest<M>;
export {};

// packages/plugin-sdk/src/runtime.d.ts
import type { PluginEvent, PluginRouteRequest, PluginStoreInfo } from "./context";
import type { PluginSettingValue } from "@scalius/shared/plugins/settings-schema";
import { type PluginHandlerInventory, type PluginLifecycleHook } from "@scalius/shared/plugins/handlers";
import { type ProviderErrorCategory } from "@scalius/shared/plugins/providers/kinds";
import type { PluginJobStepInput } from "@scalius/shared/plugins/jobs";
import { type AppErrorCode } from "@scalius/shared/plugins/app-errors";
export interface PluginContextSeed {
    plugin: {
        id: string;
        version: string;
    };
    store: PluginStoreInfo;
    settings: Record<string, PluginSettingValue>;
    objects: string[];
    inventory: PluginHandlerInventory;
    features?: string[];
}
export type PluginInvocation = {
    kind: "event";
    event: PluginEvent;
} | {
    kind: "function";
    point: string;
    input: unknown;
} | {
    kind: "provider";
    provider: string;
    operation: string;
    input: unknown;
} | {
    kind: "route";
    surface: "admin" | "public" | "webhooks";
    name: string;
    request: PluginRouteRequest;
} | {
    kind: "cron";
    name: string;
    scheduledAt: string;
} | {
    kind: "job";
    name: string;
    job: PluginJobStepInput;
} | {
    kind: "lifecycle";
    hook: PluginLifecycleHook;
    keepData?: boolean;
    feature?: string;
} | {
    kind: "migration";
    version: number;
} | {
    kind: "check";
};
export interface PluginLogRecord {
    level: "debug" | "info" | "warn" | "error";
    message: string;
    fields?: Record<string, unknown>;
}
export interface PluginInvocationResult {
    ok: boolean;
    value: unknown;
    error: {
        code: string | null;
        message: string;
        category?: ProviderErrorCategory;
        uncertain?: boolean;
    } | null;
    logs: PluginLogRecord[];
    droppedLogs: number;
}
export interface PluginRuntimeBridge {
    apiCall(operationId: string, input?: unknown): Promise<unknown>;
    objectsGet(object: string, id: string): Promise<unknown>;
    objectsGetByHandle(object: string, handle: string): Promise<unknown>;
    objectsWrite(operations: unknown[]): Promise<Array<number | null>>;
    objectsQuery(object: string, query?: unknown): Promise<unknown>;
    objectsCount(object: string, where?: unknown): Promise<number>;
    kvGet(key: string): Promise<unknown>;
    kvSet(key: string, value: unknown, options?: {
        ttlSeconds?: number;
        expectedRevision?: number | null;
    }): Promise<{
        revision: number;
    }>;
    kvDelete(key: string): Promise<void>;
    kvList(prefix?: string): Promise<string[]>;
    httpFetch(url: string, init?: unknown): Promise<{
        status: number;
        headers: Record<string, string>;
        body: string;
    }>;
    customFieldsGet(ownerType: string, ownerId: string): Promise<Record<string, unknown>>;
    customFieldsSet(ownerType: string, ownerId: string, values: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void>;
    messagingEmail(input: unknown): Promise<{
        queued: boolean;
        duplicate?: boolean;
    }>;
    messagingSms(input: unknown): Promise<{
        queued: boolean;
        duplicate?: boolean;
    }>;
    messagingNotifyStaff(input: unknown): Promise<void>;
    messagingPush?(input: unknown): Promise<{
        delivered: boolean;
    }>;
    timelineAdd?(input: unknown): Promise<{
        id: string;
    }>;
    ordersHold?(input: unknown): Promise<void>;
    ordersRelease?(input: unknown): Promise<{
        revision: number;
        replayed: boolean;
    }>;
    ordersAssessRisk?(input: unknown): Promise<void>;
    ordersRiskHistory?(orderId: unknown): Promise<{
        orders: number;
        cancelled: number;
        returnedToSender: number;
    }>;
    customerRolesGrant?(customerId: unknown, role: unknown, ref?: unknown): Promise<void>;
    customerRolesRevoke?(customerId: unknown, role: unknown): Promise<void>;
    customerRolesList?(customerId: unknown): Promise<unknown>;

        id: string;
        status: string;
    }>;

    jobsList?(options?: unknown): Promise<unknown>;
    jobsCancel?(id: unknown): Promise<unknown>;
    uploadsGet?(id: unknown): Promise<unknown>;
    uploadsRead?(id: unknown, options?: unknown): Promise<unknown>;
    uploadsDelete?(id: unknown): Promise<boolean>;
}

}

ProductSlotComponentProps<Settings, Target> : Target extends StorefrontProductCardSlot ? ProductCardSlotComponentProps<Settings, Target> : Target extends "page" ? PageComponentProps<Settings, Props> : Target extends StorefrontLayoutSlot ? LayoutSlotComponentProps<Settings, Target> : never;

export type SectionSkinComponent = (props: SectionSkinProps<any, any>) => Renderable;
export type SkinComponent = (props: any) => Renderable;
export type AppComponent = (props: ComponentProps<any, any>) => Renderable;
export declare function componentFor<const Targets extends readonly StorefrontComponentTargetName[], Settings = StorefrontResolvedSettings, Props = Record<string, unknown>>(targets: Targets, render: (props: ComponentPropsFor<Targets[number], Settings, Props>) => Renderable): AppComponent;

    sections?: Record<string, SectionComponent>;
    skins?: Record<string, SkinComponent>;
    components?: Record<string, AppComponent>;
}

    definition: StorefrontDefinition;
}

import { type Html, type Renderable } from "./html";

    children?: Renderable;
}) => Renderable;

    children?: unknown;
};

export declare const jsxDEV: typeof jsx;

    children?: unknown;
}): Html;
type AttributeValue = string | number | boolean | null | undefined;
export declare namespace JSX {
    type Element = Html;
    interface ElementChildrenAttribute {
        children: unknown;
    }
    interface IntrinsicAttributes {
        key?: string | number;
    }
    interface HtmlAttributes {
        children?: Renderable;
        class?: AttributeValue | AttributeValue[];
        className?: AttributeValue | AttributeValue[];
        style?: string | Record<string, string | number | null | undefined | false>;
        dangerouslySetInnerHTML?: {
            __html: string;
        };
        [attribute: string]: unknown;
    }
    type IntrinsicElements = Record<string, HtmlAttributes>;
}

// packages/plugin-sdk/src/testing-contract.d.ts
import type { PluginApiCallInput, PluginApiResult } from "./context";
export type ContractSchema = Record<string, unknown>;
export interface StableContractParameter {
    name: string;
    in: "path" | "query" | "header" | string;
    required?: boolean;
    schema?: ContractSchema;
    description?: string;
}
export interface StableContractOperation {
    operationId: string;
    method: string;
    path: string;
    risk: string;
    idempotency: "none" | "supported" | "required" | string;
    revision: "none" | "optional" | "required" | string;
    scopes: string[];
    scopeSets?: string[][];
    input: {
        parameters?: StableContractParameter[];
        requestBody?: {
            required?: boolean;
            content?: Record<string, {
                schema?: ContractSchema;
            }>;
        };
    } | null;
    output: ContractSchema | null;
}
export interface StableContract {
    operations: Record<string, StableContractOperation>;
    components: Record<string, ContractSchema>;
}
export declare const stableContract: StableContract;
export interface ContractCall {
    operationId: string;
    operation: StableContractOperation;
    path: Record<string, string | number>;
    query: Record<string, string | number | boolean | null>;
    body: unknown;
    idempotencyKey: string | undefined;
}
export type ContractHandler = (call: ContractCall) => unknown | Promise<unknown>;
export interface ContractApiOptions {
    handlers?: Record<string, ContractHandler>;
    scopes?: readonly string[];
    checkOutput?: boolean;
    contract?: StableContract;
}
export interface ContractApi {
    (operationId: string, input: PluginApiCallInput | undefined, call?: {
        scopes?: readonly string[];
    }): Promise<PluginApiResult>;
    readonly calls: Array<{
        operationId: string;
        input: PluginApiCallInput | undefined;
        result: PluginApiResult;
    }>;
}
interface SchemaIssue {
    path: string;
    message: string;
    missing?: string;
}
export declare function contractSchemaIssue(schemaInput: unknown, value: unknown, contract?: StableContract, path?: string): SchemaIssue | null;
export declare function createContractApi(options?: ContractApiOptions): ContractApi;
export {};

    store?: Partial<PluginStoreInfo>;
    api?: (operationId: string, input: PluginApiCallInput | undefined, call: {
        scopes: readonly string[];
    }) => PluginApiResult | Promise<PluginApiResult>;
    riskHistory?: (orderId: string) => {
        orders: number;
        cancelled: number;
        returnedToSender: number;
    };
    staff?: string[];
    fetch?: (url: string, init: unknown) => {
        status: number;
        headers?: Record<string, string>;
        body: string;
    } | Promise<{
        status: number;
        headers?: Record<string, string>;
        body: string;
    }>;
    customers?: string[];
    subscribed?: {
        email?: string[];
        sms?: string[];
    };
}

    result: PluginInvocationResult;
    job: PluginJob;
}

    value: PluginFunctionOutput<P> | null;
};

// packages/plugin-sdk/src/ui.d.ts
import type { PluginUiAction, PluginUiActionResult, PluginUiButton, PluginUiDocument, PluginUiImageSource, PluginUiNode, PluginUiRouteAction, PluginUiTarget, PluginUiTone } from "@scalius/shared/plugins";
import type { PluginRouteRequest } from "./context";
type NodeOf<T extends PluginUiNode["type"]> = Extract<PluginUiNode, {
    type: T;
}>;
type Options<T extends PluginUiNode["type"], Omitted extends string = never> = Omit<NodeOf<T>, "type" | Omitted>;
export declare const ui: {
    page: (children: PluginUiNode[], options?: Options<"page", "children">) => PluginUiDocument;
    view: (children: PluginUiNode[]) => PluginUiDocument;
    section: (title: string, children: PluginUiNode[], options?: Options<"section", "children" | "title">) => PluginUiNode;
    card: (title: string, children: PluginUiNode[], options?: Options<"card", "children" | "title">) => PluginUiNode;
    stack: (children: PluginUiNode[], options?: Options<"stack", "children">) => PluginUiNode;
    columns: (children: PluginUiNode[]) => PluginUiNode;
    heading: (text: string, level?: 2 | 3 | 4) => PluginUiNode;
    text: (text: string, options?: Options<"text", "text">) => PluginUiNode;
    badge: (text: string, tone?: PluginUiTone) => PluginUiNode;
    banner: (tone: PluginUiTone, text: string, options?: Options<"banner", "tone" | "text">) => PluginUiNode;
    keyValue: (items: NodeOf<"key-value">["items"]) => PluginUiNode;
    stat: (label: string, value: string | number, help?: string) => PluginUiNode;
    progress: (label: string, value: number, max: number, options?: Options<"progress", "label" | "value" | "max">) => PluginUiNode;
    table: (options: Options<"table">) => PluginUiNode;
    empty: (title: string, options?: Options<"empty-state", "title">) => PluginUiNode;
    image: (src: PluginUiImageSource, alt: string, options?: Options<"image", "src" | "alt">) => PluginUiNode;
    link: (text: string, to: PluginUiTarget) => PluginUiNode;
    divider: () => PluginUiNode;
    button: (label: string, action: PluginUiAction, options?: Omit<PluginUiButton, "type" | "label" | "action">) => PluginUiButton;
    form: (action: PluginUiRouteAction, submitLabel: string, children: PluginUiNode[], options?: Options<"form", "action" | "submitLabel" | "children">) => PluginUiNode;
    textField: (name: string, label: string, options?: Options<"text-field", "name" | "label">) => PluginUiNode;
    textarea: (name: string, label: string, options?: Options<"textarea", "name" | "label">) => PluginUiNode;
    numberField: (name: string, label: string, options?: Options<"number-field", "name" | "label">) => PluginUiNode;
    select: (name: string, label: string, options: NodeOf<"select">["options"], rest?: Options<"select", "name" | "label" | "options">) => PluginUiNode;
    checkbox: (name: string, label: string, options?: Options<"checkbox", "name" | "label">) => PluginUiNode;
    dateField: (name: string, label: string, options?: Options<"date-field", "name" | "label">) => PluginUiNode;
    fileField: (name: string, label: string, options?: Options<"file-field", "name" | "label">) => PluginUiNode;
    field: (attribute: string, options?: {
        choices?: Array<{
            value: string;
            label: string;
        }>;
    }) => PluginUiNode;
    route: (route: string, options?: Omit<PluginUiRouteAction, "kind" | "route">) => PluginUiRouteAction;
    navigate: (to: PluginUiTarget) => PluginUiAction;
    cart: {
        addLine: (variantId: string, quantity?: number) => PluginUiAction;
        applyCode: (code: string) => PluginUiAction;
        setAttribute: (key: string, value: string) => PluginUiAction;
    };
    to: {
        resource: (resource: Extract<PluginUiTarget, {
            kind: "resource";
        }>["resource"], id: string) => PluginUiTarget;
        page: (page: string, query?: Record<string, string>) => PluginUiTarget;
        external: (href: string) => PluginUiTarget;
        store: (path: string) => PluginUiTarget;
    };
    result: (result: Omit<PluginUiActionResult, "toast"> & {
        toast?: string | PluginUiActionResult["toast"];
    }) => PluginUiActionResult;
};
export interface PluginAdminContext {
    resource: string | null;
    id: string | null;
    ids: string[];
    action: string | null;
    cursor: string | null;
    values: Record<string, unknown>;
    locale: PluginAdminLocale;
}
export type PluginAdminLocale = "en" | "bn";
export declare function adminLocale(request: Pick<PluginRouteRequest, "headers">): PluginAdminLocale;
export declare function adminNumber(value: number, locale: PluginAdminLocale): string;
export declare function adminContext(request: PluginRouteRequest): PluginAdminContext;
export declare function wantsPage(request: PluginRouteRequest): boolean;
export {};

export type EventRepresentation<T extends EventRepresentationType = EventRepresentationType> = z.output<(typeof EVENT_REPRESENTATION_SCHEMAS)[T]>;

export declare function verifyWebhookSignature(options: {
    secret: string;
    header: string | null | undefined;
    body: string;
    nowSeconds?: number;
    toleranceSeconds?: number;
}): Promise<boolean>;

// packages/shared/src/fulfilment.d.ts
export declare const FULFILLMENT_RECORD_STATUSES: readonly [
    "active",
    "voided"
];

// packages/shared/src/line-properties.d.ts
export interface AppLineInputDeclaration {
    key: string;
    label: string;
    help?: string;
    type: LineInputFieldType;
    maxLength?: number;
    options?: ReadonlyArray<{
        value: string;
        label: string;
        confirm?: LineInputConfirm;
    }>;
    bulk?: LineInputBulk;
    accept?: readonly BuyerUploadType[];
    maxBytes?: number;
    maxFiles?: number;
    textRevision?: string;
    decision?: {
        proceedWith?: string;
    };
    products?: AppLineInputGate;
    variants?: AppLineInputGate;
}
export interface AppLineInputGate {
    customField: string;
}
export interface LineInputBulk {
    apply?: {
        label: string;
        value?: string;
    };
    clear?: {
        label: string;
    };
}
export interface LineInputConfirm {
    text: string;
    title?: string;
    agree?: string;
}
export type LineInputFieldType = "select" | "text" | "textarea" | "checkbox" | "file";

// packages/shared/src/plugins/buyer-events.d.ts
export type AnyBuyerEvent = BuyerEvent<"page_viewed"> | BuyerEvent<"product_viewed"> | BuyerEvent<"collection_viewed"> | BuyerEvent<"search_submitted"> | BuyerEvent<"product_added_to_cart"> | BuyerEvent<"product_removed_from_cart"> | BuyerEvent<"cart_viewed"> | BuyerEvent<"checkout_started"> | BuyerEvent<"checkout_contact_submitted"> | BuyerEvent<"checkout_address_submitted"> | BuyerEvent<"checkout_shipping_submitted"> | BuyerEvent<"checkout_payment_submitted"> | BuyerEvent<"checkout_completed"> | BuyerEvent<"app_event">;
export type AnyPixelEvent = PixelEvent<"page_viewed"> | PixelEvent<"product_viewed"> | PixelEvent<"collection_viewed"> | PixelEvent<"search_submitted"> | PixelEvent<"product_added_to_cart"> | PixelEvent<"product_removed_from_cart"> | PixelEvent<"cart_viewed"> | PixelEvent<"checkout_started"> | PixelEvent<"checkout_contact_submitted"> | PixelEvent<"checkout_address_submitted"> | PixelEvent<"checkout_shipping_submitted"> | PixelEvent<"checkout_payment_submitted"> | PixelEvent<"checkout_completed"> | PixelEvent<"app_event">;
export declare const BUYER_EVENT_NAMES: readonly [
    "page_viewed",
    "product_viewed",
    "collection_viewed",
    "search_submitted",
    "product_added_to_cart",
    "product_removed_from_cart",
    "cart_viewed",
    "checkout_started",
    "checkout_contact_submitted",
    "checkout_address_submitted",
    "checkout_shipping_submitted",
    "checkout_payment_submitted",
    "checkout_completed"
];
export interface BuyerAddress {
    name: string | null;
    line1: string | null;
    line2: string | null;
    locality: string | null;
    adminArea: string | null;
    postalCode: string | null;
    countryCode: string | null;
}
export interface BuyerCheckout {
    id: string | null;
    lines: BuyerLine[];
    itemCount: number;
    total: BuyerMoney | null;
}
export interface BuyerConsent {
    analytics: boolean;
    marketing: boolean;
    saleOfData: boolean;
}
export interface BuyerContact {
    email: string | null;
    phone: string | null;
}
export interface BuyerEvent<N extends PixelEventName = PixelEventName> {
    id: string;
    name: N;
    timestamp: string;
    data: BuyerEventData[N];
}
export interface BuyerEventContext {
    page: {
        url: string;
        path: string;
        title: string;
        referrer: string;
    };
    language: string;
    currency: string;
    visitorId: string | null;
}
export interface BuyerEventData {
    page_viewed: Record<string, never>;
    product_viewed: {
        merchandise: BuyerMerchandise;
        category: string | null;
    };
    collection_viewed: {
        collection: {
            kind: "category" | "brand" | "collection";
            id: string | null;
            title: string;
        };
        products: BuyerMerchandise[];
    };
    search_submitted: {
        query: string;
        results: BuyerMerchandise[];
    };
    product_added_to_cart: {
        line: BuyerLine;
    };
    product_removed_from_cart: {
        line: BuyerLine;
    };
    cart_viewed: {
        lines: BuyerLine[];
        total: BuyerMoney | null;
    };
    checkout_started: {
        checkout: BuyerCheckout;
    };
    checkout_contact_submitted: {
        checkout: BuyerCheckout;
        contact: BuyerContact;
    };
    checkout_address_submitted: {
        checkout: BuyerCheckout;
        address: BuyerAddress;
    };
    checkout_shipping_submitted: {
        checkout: BuyerCheckout;
        shipping: {
            kind: "delivery" | "pickup";
            methodId: string | null;
            title: string;
            cost: BuyerMoney | null;
        };
    };
    checkout_payment_submitted: {
        checkout: BuyerCheckout;
        payment: {
            method: string;
        };
    };
    checkout_completed: {
        checkout: BuyerCheckout;
        order: {
            id: string;
        };
    };
    app_event: {
        app: string;
        name: string;
        payload: unknown;
    };
}
export type BuyerEventName = "page_viewed" | "product_viewed" | "collection_viewed" | "search_submitted" | "product_added_to_cart" | "product_removed_from_cart" | "cart_viewed" | "checkout_started" | "checkout_contact_submitted" | "checkout_address_submitted" | "checkout_shipping_submitted" | "checkout_payment_submitted" | "checkout_completed";
export interface BuyerLine {
    merchandise: BuyerMerchandise;
    quantity: number;
}
export interface BuyerMerchandise {
    productId: string;
    variantId: string | null;
    title: string;
    price: BuyerMoney | null;
}
export interface BuyerMoney {
    amountMinor: number;
    currency: string;
}
export declare function experimentVariant(visitorId: string | null, experiment: string, variants: readonly string[] | Readonly<Record<string, number>>): string;
export declare const PIXEL_EVENT_NAMES: readonly [
    "page_viewed",
    "product_viewed",
    "collection_viewed",
    "search_submitted",
    "product_added_to_cart",
    "product_removed_from_cart",
    "cart_viewed",
    "checkout_started",
    "checkout_contact_submitted",
    "checkout_address_submitted",
    "checkout_shipping_submitted",
    "checkout_payment_submitted",
    "checkout_completed",
    "app_event"
];
export interface PixelApi {
    pluginId: string;
    settings: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean | null>>;
    consent: Readonly<BuyerConsent>;
    visitorId(): string | null;
    on<N extends PixelEventName>(name: N, listener: (event: PixelEvent<N>) => void): () => void;
    on(name: "*", listener: (event: AnyPixelEvent) => void): () => void;
}
export type PixelConsentCategory = "marketing" | "analytics";
export type PixelEvent<N extends PixelEventName = PixelEventName> = BuyerEvent<N> & {
    context: BuyerEventContext;
};
export type PixelEventName = "page_viewed" | "product_viewed" | "collection_viewed" | "search_submitted" | "product_added_to_cart" | "product_removed_from_cart" | "cart_viewed" | "checkout_started" | "checkout_contact_submitted" | "checkout_address_submitted" | "checkout_shipping_submitted" | "checkout_payment_submitted" | "checkout_completed" | "app_event";
export interface PixelModule {
    abi: 1;
    setup(api: PixelApi): void;
}

            cart: z.ZodObject<{
                currency: z.ZodString;
                decimalPlaces: z.ZodNumber;
                storeCurrency: z.ZodObject<{
                    code: z.ZodString;
                    decimalPlaces: z.ZodNumber;
                }, z.core.$strict>;
                channel: z.ZodEnum<{
                    admin: "admin";
                    online_store: "online_store";
                    pos: "pos";
                    agent: "agent";
                    app: "app";
                }>;
                salesChannelId: z.ZodDefault<z.ZodString>;
                lines: z.ZodArray<z.ZodObject<{
                    lineId: z.ZodString;
                    productId: z.ZodString;
                    variantId: z.ZodString;
                    sku: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
                    title: z.ZodString;
                    quantity: z.ZodNumber;
                    unitPriceMinor: z.ZodNumber;
                    lineTotalMinor: z.ZodNumber;
                    categoryIds: z.ZodArray<z.ZodString>;
                    collectionIds: z.ZodArray<z.ZodString>;
                    brandId: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
                    properties: z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodString>;
                    inputRevisions: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodObject<{
                        answered: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
                        current: z.ZodString;
                    }, z.core.$strict>>>;
                    bundle: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodNullable<z.ZodObject<{
                        id: z.ZodString;
                        title: z.ZodString;
                        kind: z.ZodEnum<{
                            expand: "expand";
                            merge: "merge";
                        }>;
                        anchor: z.ZodBoolean;
                    }, z.core.$strict>>>;
                    requiresShipping: z.ZodDefault<z.ZodBoolean>;
                    weightGrams: z.ZodDefault<z.ZodNullable<z.ZodNumber>>;
                    shippingProfileId: z.ZodDefault<z.ZodString>;
                    purchaseOptions: z.ZodDefault<z.ZodArray<z.ZodString>>;
                    addOns: z.ZodDefault<z.ZodArray<z.ZodObject<{
                        appId: z.ZodString;
                        title: z.ZodString;
                        amountMinor: z.ZodNumber;
                    }, z.core.$strict>>>;
                }, z.core.$strict>>;
                subtotalMinor: z.ZodNumber;
                customer: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodObject<{
                    id: z.ZodString;
                    tags: z.ZodArray<z.ZodString>;
                    segmentIds: z.ZodDefault<z.ZodArray<z.ZodString>>;
                    orderCount: z.ZodNumber;
                    totalSpentMinor: z.ZodNumber;
                    roles: z.ZodDefault<z.ZodArray<z.ZodObject<{
                        role: z.ZodString;
                        ref: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
                    }, z.core.$strict>>>;
                }, z.core.$strict>>;
                destination: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodObject<{
                    country: z.ZodString;
                    city: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
                    zone: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
                }, z.core.$strict>>;
                discountCodes: z.ZodArray<z.ZodString>;
                market: z.ZodObject<{
                    id: z.ZodString;
                    country: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
                }, z.core.$strict>;
                attributes: z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodString>;
            }, z.core.$strict>;
            reads: z.ZodDefault<z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodUnion<readonly [
                z.ZodNullable<z.ZodObject<{
                    revision: z.ZodNumber;
                    data: z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodUnknown>;
                }, z.core.$strict>>,
                z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodObject<{
                    revision: z.ZodNumber;
                    data: z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodUnknown>;
                }, z.core.$strict>>,
                z.ZodArray<z.ZodObject<{
                    revision: z.ZodNumber;
                    data: z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodUnknown>;
                    id: z.ZodString;
                }, z.core.$strict>>
            ]>>>;
            now: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodISODateTime>;
            language: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        readonly output: z.ZodObject<{
            operations: z.ZodArray<z.ZodDiscriminatedUnion<[
                z.ZodObject<{
                    op: z.ZodLiteral<"expand">;
                    lineId: z.ZodString;
                    title: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
                    components: z.ZodArray<z.ZodObject<{
                        variantId: z.ZodString;
                        quantity: z.ZodNumber;
                    }, z.core.$strict>>;
                }, z.core.$strict>,
                z.ZodObject<{
                    op: z.ZodLiteral<"merge">;
                    lineIds: z.ZodArray<z.ZodString>;
                    title: z.ZodString;
                }, z.core.$strict>
            ], "op">>;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
    };

export type PluginFunctionAnswer<P extends PluginRunPoint> = z.input<(typeof PLUGIN_RUN_SCHEMAS)[P]["output"]>;
export type PluginFunctionInput<P extends PluginRunPoint> = z.infer<(typeof PLUGIN_RUN_SCHEMAS)[P]["input"]>;
export type PluginFunctionInputDraft<P extends PluginRunPoint> = z.input<(typeof PLUGIN_RUN_SCHEMAS)[P]["input"]>;
export type PluginFunctionOutput<P extends PluginRunPoint> = z.infer<(typeof PLUGIN_RUN_SCHEMAS)[P]["output"]>;
export type PluginFunctionRunInput<P extends PluginRunPoint> = Omit<PluginFunctionInput<P>, "now" | "language"> & {
    now: string;
    language: string;
};
export type PluginRunPoint = "cart.transform" | "cart.line_prices" | "cart.validate" | "checkout.discounts" | "checkout.shipping_rates" | "checkout.payment_methods" | "buyer.ui";

// packages/shared/src/plugins/handlers.d.ts
export interface PluginDormantInventory {
    events: string[];
    functions: string[];
    extensions?: string[];
    routes: {
        admin: string[];
        public: string[];
        webhooks: string[];
    };
    cron: string[];
    providers: PluginProviderInventory[];
}
export interface PluginHandlerInventory {
    events: string[];
    functions: string[];
    extensions?: string[];
    routes: {
        admin: string[];
        public: string[];
        webhooks: string[];
    };
    cron: string[];
    jobs?: string[];
    providers: PluginProviderInventory[];
    migrations?: string[];
    dormant?: PluginDormantInventory;
}
export type PluginLifecycleHook = "install" | "activate" | "deactivate" | "uninstall" | "enable" | "disable";
export interface PluginProviderInventory {
    id: string;
    kind: ProviderKind;
    operations: string[];
}

// packages/shared/src/plugins/jobs.d.ts
export interface PluginJob {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    status: PluginJobStatus;
    progress: {
        done: number;
        total: number;
    };
    message: string | null;
    error: string | null;
    steps: number;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    finishedAt: string | null;
}
export type PluginJobStatus = "failed" | "running" | "done";
export interface PluginJobStepInput {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    input: unknown;
    state: unknown;
    step: number;
}
export interface PluginJobStepResult {
    state?: unknown;
    progress?: {
        done: number;
        total: number;
    };
    message?: string | null;
    done?: boolean;
}

// packages/shared/src/plugins/manifest.d.ts
export declare function parsePluginManifest(value: unknown): {
    ok: true;
    manifest: PluginManifest;
} | {
    ok: false;
    issues: Array<{
        path: string;
        message: string;
    }>;
};
export type PluginFunctionPoint = "cart.transform" | "cart.line_prices" | "cart.validate" | "checkout.discounts" | "checkout.shipping_rates" | "checkout.payment_methods";
export declare function pluginGrantRequest(declared: PluginManifest, features?: Iterable<string>): PluginGrantRequest;
export interface PluginGrantRequest {
    scopes: PluginScope[];
    allowedHosts: string[];
    events: EventTopicFilter[];
    functions: PluginFunctionPoint[];
    extensions: StorefrontUiSlot[];
    blockingFunctions: PluginFunctionPoint[];
    providers: PluginProviderKind[];
    publicRoutes: boolean;
    storefrontScripts: string[];
    storefrontSurfaces: boolean;

}; }; };
export type ProviderImplementation<K extends ProviderKind, C> = {
    [M in RequiredOperation<K>]: Operation<K, M, C, "sync-or-async">;
} & {
    [M in OptionalOperation<K>]?: Operation<K, M, C, "sync-or-async">;
};
export type ProviderOperationInput<K extends ProviderKind, M extends keyof Ports[K]> = z.output<Schemas<K, M>["input"]>;
export type ProviderOperationOutput<K extends ProviderKind, M extends keyof Ports[K]> = z.output<Schemas<K, M>["output"]>;
type RequiredOperation<K extends ProviderKind> = (typeof PROVIDER_OPERATIONS)[K]["required"][number] & keyof Ports[K];
type Schemas<K extends ProviderKind, M extends keyof Ports[K]> = Ports[K][M] extends {
    input: z.ZodType;
    output: z.ZodType;
} ? Ports[K][M] : never;

// packages/shared/src/plugins/providers/amount.d.ts
export type ProviderWireAmount = { minor: number; decimal: string; };

// packages/shared/src/plugins/providers/common.d.ts
export type ProviderConnection = { ok: boolean; message: string; };
export type ProviderHttpRequest = { method: string; rawBody: string; headers: Record<string, string>; query: Record<string, string>; };

// packages/shared/src/plugins/providers/courier-history.d.ts
export type CourierHistory = { riskLevel: "unknown" | "low" | "medium" | "high"; phone: string; totalParcels: number; delivered: number; cancelled: number; successRate?: number | undefined; cancelRate?: number | undefined; customerTag?: string | undefined; providerStatus?: string | undefined; message?: string | undefined; couriers?: Record<string, { totalParcels: number; delivered: number; cancelled: number; }> | undefined; };

export declare class ProviderError extends Error {
    readonly category: ProviderErrorCategory;
    readonly uncertain: boolean;
    readonly code: string;
    constructor(category: ProviderErrorCategory, message: string, options?: {
        uncertain?: boolean;
        code?: string;
    });
}
export type ProviderErrorCategory = "declined" | "retryable" | "misconfigured";

export interface StorefrontRenderContext {
    store: {
        name: string;

    };
    page: {
        kind: "home" | "landing" | "product" | "listing" | "search" | "cart" | "page" | "other";
        path: string;
    };
    assets: string;
    preview: boolean;
    app?: {
        settings: Record<string, string | number | boolean | string[] | null>;
    };
}
interface SkinJobBase {
    id: string;
    kind: "skin";
    component: string;
    settings: StorefrontResolvedSettings;
}
export type StorefrontSkinJob = (SkinJobBase & {
    target: "header";
    data: StorefrontHeaderData;
}) | (SkinJobBase & {
    target: "footer";
    data: StorefrontFooterData;
}) | (SkinJobBase & {
    target: "product_card";
    data: StorefrontProductCardData;
}) | (SkinJobBase & {
    target: "product_main";
    data: StorefrontProductPage;
}) | (SkinJobBase & {
    target: "listing_main";
    data: StorefrontListingPage;
}) | (SkinJobBase & {
    target: `section:${string}`;
    instanceId: string;
    data: StorefrontSectionSkinData;
});
export type StorefrontRenderJob = {
    id: string;
    kind: "section";
    objects?: Record<string, {
        records: Array<{
            id: string;
            handle: string | null;
            data: Record<string, unknown>;
        }>;
        fields: Record<string, string>;
    }>;
    type: string;
    instanceId: string;
    settings: StorefrontResolvedSettings;
} | StorefrontSkinJob | StorefrontComponentJob;
interface ComponentJobBase {
    id: string;
    kind: "component";
    type: string;
    settings: StorefrontResolvedSettings;
}
export type StorefrontComponentJob = (ComponentJobBase & {
    target: StorefrontProductSlot;
    instanceId: string;
    data: StorefrontProductPage;
    objects?: Record<string, {
        records: Array<{
            id: string;
            handle: string | null;
            data: Record<string, unknown>;
        }>;
        fields: Record<string, string>;
    }>;
}) | (ComponentJobBase & {
    target: StorefrontProductCardSlot;
    data: StorefrontProductCardData;
}) | (ComponentJobBase & {
    target: Exclude<StorefrontComponentSlot, StorefrontProductSlot | StorefrontProductCardSlot>;
}) | (ComponentJobBase & {
    target: "page";
    props: Record<string, unknown>;
});
export interface StorefrontRenderBatch {
    abi: typeof STOREFRONT_COMPONENT_ABI;
    context: StorefrontRenderContext;
    jobs: StorefrontRenderJob[];
}
export type StorefrontRenderResult = {
    id: string;
    html: string;
} | {
    id: string;
    error: string;
};
export interface StorefrontRenderReply {
    abi: typeof STOREFRONT_COMPONENT_ABI;
    results: StorefrontRenderResult[];
}
export interface StorefrontModule {
    abi: typeof STOREFRONT_COMPONENT_ABI;
    render(batch: StorefrontRenderBatch): StorefrontRenderReply;
}
export declare const STOREFRONT_SEARCH_SUGGESTIONS_MAX = 7;
export interface StorefrontSearchSuggestion {
    productId: string;
    title: string;
    url: string;
    image: StorefrontImage | null;
    price: StorefrontMoney;
    compareAt: StorefrontMoney | null;
    priceVaries: boolean;
    available: boolean;
}
export interface StorefrontCartLine {
    lineKey: string;
    productId: string;
    variantId: string;
    title: string;
    quantity: number;
    unitPrice: StorefrontMoney;
    inputs: string[];
    answers: Record<string, string>;
    undecided?: string[];
    addOns: Array<{
        title: string;
        amount: StorefrontMoney;
    }>;
}
export type StorefrontLineInputResult = {
    ok: true;
    changed: number;
} | {
    ok: false;
    reason: "cancelled" | "unknown_input" | "not_applicable" | "failed";
};
export type StorefrontDecideResult = {
    ok: true;
    changed: number;
} | {
    ok: false;
    reason: "cancelled" | "undecided" | "failed";
};
export interface StorefrontIslandContext {
    pluginId: string;
    assets: string;
    language: string;
    currency: string;
    formatMoney(money: {
        amountMinor: number;
        currency: string;
    }): string;
    openCart(): void;
    quickAdd(productUrl: string): Promise<boolean>;
    addLine?(line: {
        variantId: string;
        quantity?: number;
        purchaseOption?: string;
        inputs?: Record<string, string>;
    }): Promise<boolean>;
    onCart(listener: (cart: {
        count: number;
        total: StorefrontMoney;
    }) => void): () => void;
    cart?: {
        lines(): Promise<StorefrontCartLine[]>;
        setLineInput(change: {
            key: string;
            value: string | null;
            lineKeys?: string[];
        }): Promise<StorefrontLineInputResult>;
        decide?(): Promise<StorefrontDecideResult>;
    };
    fetchApp(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<Response>;
    signedIn(): boolean;
    searchSuggestions?(query: string, options?: {
        limit?: number;
        signal?: AbortSignal;
    }): Promise<StorefrontSearchSuggestion[]>;
    product?: {
        state(): StorefrontProductState;
        onChange(listener: (state: StorefrontProductState) => void): () => void;
    };
    events?: StorefrontIslandEvents;
    visitorId?(): string | null;
}
export interface StorefrontIslandEvents {
    on<N extends PixelEventName>(name: N, listener: (event: BuyerEvent<N>) => void, options?: {
        replay?: boolean;
    }): () => void;
    publish(name: string, payload?: unknown): boolean;
}
export interface StorefrontIslandModule<Props = unknown> {
    abi: typeof STOREFRONT_COMPONENT_ABI;
    mount(element: HTMLElement, props: Props, context: StorefrontIslandContext): void | (() => void);
}

export declare function storefrontExtensionBase(pluginId: string, release: string): string;
export declare function parseStorefrontExtensionPath(pathname: string): {
    pluginId: string;
    release: string;
    path: string;
} | null;
export declare function storefrontIsolateId(pluginId: string, release: string): string;
export {};
